# ClawVille — 3D Structure

**Last Audited: 2026-08-02 (Land P1 public, shell-aware structures).**
`StructureHydrator` now reads the public, active-only
`GET /api/land/structures/public` feed for every player's building, polls the
60-second cache interval, and overlays the authenticated owner's uncached land
response so recent local mutations remain responsive. A structure resolves its
`home.glb` or `shop.glb` from the server-returned shell key with the explicit
`coastal-cottage` fallback; `levelScale(level)` remains the progression scale.

Model normalization now treats footprint and height independently. The base
scale is `min((parcelSize * 0.62 / widestXZ) * levelScale,
parcelSize * 1.50 / height)`, so the independent height cap clamps the final
level-scaled result. Bounds below are transformed native GLB
bounds; fitted dimensions use the smallest shell-rendered parcel (founder,
1,216 world units) at the first premium levels:

| Premium asset | Native X × Y × Z | widest XZ / height | Mesh cost | Lv4 fitted X × Y × Z | Lv5 fitted X × Y × Z | Limiter |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `premium-tower/home.glb` | 40,634 × 180,218.5 × 44,814 | 44,814 / 180,218.5 | 986 triangles, 6 primitives | 411.2586 × 1,824 × 453.5646 | 411.2586 × 1,824 × 453.5646 | height |
| `premium-mall/shop.glb` | 66,844.6788 × 55,053.4790 × 40,632.7192 | 66,844.6788 / 55,053.4790 | 2,364 triangles, 4 primitives | 853.8144 × 703.2041 × 519.0062 | 942.4 × 776.1635 × 572.8545 | footprint |

Palette tinting clones only each rendered mesh's geometry, stamps a preset
swatch into vertex colors, and reuses one parent-owned vertex-color
`MeshStandardMaterial`. Static shell groups disable matrix auto-update; owned
geometry and the shared tint material are disposed on unmount, while `useGLTF`
cache assets are never disposed. This keeps the current small-occupancy draw
cost compatible with a later chunk-merge implementation.

**Manual overlap check:** on `/game`, inspect allowed Lv4/Lv5 founder-parcel
premium shells from several camera angles against all ten procedural
`land-founder-apartments.tsx` skyline pieces. Confirm no footprint or roofline
interpenetration. The apartment geometry is intentionally unchanged and this
visual sign-off remains with the orchestrator.

**Prior Last Audited: 2026-07-30 (Persistent world-stage P4 activity overlay slot).**
`/activity/:activityId/:roomId` now joins the `(world)` route group and registers
an empty resident `activity` stage slot while Reef Race and Bumper Shells retain
their room-keyed page-layer WebGL canvases. The shared stage pauses only after
the activity transition reaches idle; readiness is bound to the requested room
and acknowledged after the activity canvas paints or a room-scoped terminal
surface is visible. Returns use the stage fade and hold the cover until the
opaque activity subtree actually unmounts; remote bodies reporting
`at-activity` render idle with an `· in an activity` suffix. **Drift note:** P4 citations were
frozen pre-P3; the live stage already contained the unified controller, Kelp
slot, and v42 protocol baseline, so the activity slot was applied to those
landed roles.

**Prior Last Audited: 2026-07-30 (Persistent world-stage P3 Kelp cutover).** `/game`,
`/cove`, and page-only `/kelp` now share the unkeyed `(world)` stage Canvas.
Kelp is a resident `StageHostedKelpScene` slot whose scene graph, camera,
controls, activation reset, warmup, health/recovery, and resource ledger remain
owned by the stage. Returns are fades, not Canvas reloads. The route-scoped
HUD stays DOM-owned, while the shared player-capability controller runs under
`KELP_POLICY` (no jump/sprint/emotes/interact; camera-relative movement and
touch retained). `/kelp` uploads `at-kelp` presence while the world body remains
co-present. The Kelp scene intentionally uses `Fog(0x0d4552, 1100, 3200)` with
camera far 10,000; the world-only fog-far/camera-far equality rule does not
apply to this scene-scoped atmosphere.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-30 (**Ansem exclusive avatar + model-intrinsic sword attachments.**) New registry-only model key `ansem` renders `/avatars/ansem.vrm` at the male-humanoid 297-wu target with `faceYaw: Math.PI`, hidden picker entry, preview turnaround, and fox 2D fallback; it remains absent from shared `AGENT_MODELS`, so only a manual DB grant can assign it. Animator ID `ansem` uses the canonicalized/stripped Meshy-native action-334 idle while stripping idle/walk/run position tracks for the 0.01-scale Meshy rig. `character-attachments.ts` adds the animator-owned static-prop path used by every VRM render context: one module cache per GLB URL, `clone(true)` per animator, native-avatar-height/asset-longest-axis/bone-scale-aware sizing, wrapper-only culling disable, and transition-only reparenting between right-hand idle and upper-chest moving anchors. One-shots and non-idle surface overrides count as moving; teardown removes the wrapper without disposing shared cached geometry/materials. Anchor transforms were live-tuned against the built preview via the new `/preview/hermes` QC handle `window.__PREVIEW_VRM_SCENE` (idle: right-hand grip, rot X 180°, pos Y 61 bone-units; moving: upper-chest back carry, rot Z 40°, pos [0,−4,−16] — upperChest-local +Z is the character's FRONT on this rig). Non-Ansem animators stop at a table miss and allocate/load nothing. Cosmetic render change only: no economy, state, route, action, protocol, shader, or per-frame allocation change.

**Prior Last edit / Last Audited:** 2026-07-27 (**Gateway approach no longer overrides walking activity.**) The three gateway destination actions now stamp only the serialized activity-emoji field and leave the simulated body in the walking state, so the 5 Hz movement gate no longer skips it for the 8 to 20 second activity window. Arrivals at the two gateway destinations keep their agent-owned route marker across the arrival path reset so a parked agent body is not re-planned by the ambient wander; teaching-building and REST-move arrivals keep their existing release behavior. The emoji field is not rendered by the current client. No client geometry, collider shape, render path, SSE wire shape, NPC speed, or Cove settlement change.

**Last edit / Last Audited:** 2026-07-26 (**Persistent world-stage P1c presence, navigation, and soak implementation; ALL serial gates GREEN 2026-07-27 under the v4.1 calibration (see docs/world-stage-p1c-notes.md).**) `WorldPresence` is mounted beside `WorldStageRoot` in `app/(world)/layout.tsx`, so `/game` owns active 5 Hz position uploads while `/cove` preserves the same room/SSE session with a frozen-position `at-cove` heartbeat no faster than every 10 seconds; returning to `/game` resets the velocity baseline before activity derivation. The world-stage bridge now buffers one pre-handler request for at most 5 seconds with route-generation invalidation, explicit stage mount registration, Strict Mode-safe install generations, guarded expiry fallbacks, and phase-aware ADOPT/execute-now/SUPERSEDE ownership. The route probe adds lifetime join/stream accounting, a deterministic pre-install navigation, honest backend-specific renderer counters, configurable loops, and a 60-loop soak plateau lane. **Deliberate dual-GLB note:** `cove-interior.tsx` intentionally preloads both the optimized interior and the 58 KB fallback; the fallback is required by the live five-second FPS auto-switch and is not a duplicate-download defect. No texture eviction, game/economy, room route, NPC simulation, or Cove settlement change.

**Last edit / Last Audited:** 2026-07-26 (**Directed-route wall-slide persistence; driver-owned re-routing.**) Server-directed agent routes are tracked by path-array identity and retain their route through collider grazes using the existing collision-free clamp/wall-slide. The Puppeteer gate now has a separate exact-30-round-trip real-route lane in addition to both synthetic lanes. **Drift note:** P1a prose incorrectly carried `[0.5,0.65]`; live code and the canonical low-end DPR remain `[0.55,0.7]`. No stream/presence, API/economy/protocol, texture-eviction, or low-end-tier change. See `docs/world-stage-p1b-notes.md`.

**Prior Last edit / Last Audited:** 2026-07-25 (**Persistent world-stage P0b compatibility/health/ledger/probe pass; production bundle and both scripted renderer lanes pass, reviewer visual sign-off pending.**) The isolated `/perf/stage?stage=1` proof now keeps `alpha`, `beta`, and a representative `cove-spike` resident under one unkeyed R3F v9 Canvas. The Cove spike uses the real optimized KTX2 interior GLB and represents Cove's `MeshStandardMaterial`, canvas-textured transparent `MeshBasicMaterial`, and three light classes/objects without importing live Cove stores, avatar/control/HUD, or economy behavior. Renderer health now uses the canonical GPU detector; watches WebGPU device loss and sustained uncaptured errors; performs at most one same-canvas renderer recreation before session-sticky WebGL fallback; tracks WebGL context loss/restore; and clears input, clamps the next delta, resyncs R3F size, and invalidates on visibility/pageshow wake. Slot frame counters stay module-local and sample into Zustand at 4 Hz. A slot-root ledger reports geometry source bytes, exact compressed mip payloads, estimated uncompressed texture/render-target bytes, and known uncertainty instead of calling texture counts VRAM. Both saved 100-transition production-bundle probes pass with one Canvas mount, 100 frozen hidden windows, zero hidden frame/camera/store violations, zero transition errors, zero listener delta, zero recoveries, and post-warmup heap growth of 1.241% WebGPU / 1.102% WebGL; both honestly retain an anonymous-auth 401 and `THREE.Clock` deprecation warning. Root build and web strict TypeScript pass. Live `/game`, `/cove`, `/kelp`, `/arena`, and `/activity/**` routes remain untouched; no route-group cutover or real-scene migration has occurred, and reviewer/Fable visual approval is not claimed.

**Last edit:** 2026-07-23 (Cove-exit loading-freeze hotfix — return-from-subroute 90% hang). `/game`, `/cove`, `/kelp`, and the Reef Race arena are SEPARATE route trees, each with its own R3F `<Canvas>` + WebGPU context; returning to `/game` from any of them always COLD-BOOTS a brand-new world Canvas (re-parse VRMs, re-upload textures, re-compile shaders) — that is the full loading screen on every return. The freeze: `__W3D_CANVAS_READY` was set exactly once in a one-shot rAF (`kickRenderLoop`), while `SeaLoadingScreen` re-zeroes all `__W3D_*` bridge flags on every mount; on an SPA route RETURN the already-downloaded world chunk can set canvas-ready BEFORE the remounting loader zeroes it back, stranding it false forever → `__W3D_READY` (canvas && textures) never flips → bar hangs ~85-90% until the 45s force-dismiss. Fix: a standalone rAF self-heal watchdog in `kickRenderLoop` re-asserts `__W3D_CANVAS_READY` + re-runs `markWorldReadyIfUploadsDone()` every frame until `__W3D_READY` (guards: stop on ready / canvas-disconnected / 60s ceiling; never touches `__W3D_TEXTURES_READY` so it cannot dismiss earlier than a clean boot). **TODO (real fix, NOT done here):** keep the world Canvas alive across route navigation (pause instead of destroy) so returning from a sub-experience does not cold-reboot the world at all.

**Last edit:** 2026-07-23 (Biggie inverted-arms ROOT CAUSE + fix — HARD RULE for all future Meshy rigs): founder caught arms folded INSIDE the body ("swapped shoulders" while walking, armless at idle) on the laptop. Root cause: the turnaround images' arms were ~30° below horizontal → Meshy rigged the mesh as-drawn → the skeleton REST pose had arms drooped 30° (arm-joint rest quats ~44-47° vs working Adinero's ~15-21°) → VRM 1.0 + the Mixamo-retarget animator assume rest == true T-pose → every retargeted arm rotation applied ~30-40° off, folding arms inward. **The armspan/height QC floor of 0.95 exists precisely for this and was measured (0.867/0.900/0.904) then waved through — NEVER ship a Meshy rig below the 0.95 floor.** Repair without regeneration: `scripts/hermes-pipeline/fix-rig-tpose.mjs` rotates Left/RightArm rest quats so shoulder→wrist is exactly ±X (IBMs untouched — mesh skins up into true T-pose), then re-inject VRM + re-optimize; `/avatars/biggie.vrm` re-baked at the same path → registry bumped to `?v=2` (asset-cache-bust rule — a stale cache faked a failed fix during verification). Second process rule: **animated front-view (az=270) idle+walk screenshots are now the minimum rig QC** — the side-profile shots used the first time cannot distinguish arms-inward from arms-at-sides (`/preview/hermes?az=` param added for scripted angles).

**R18f Last edit / Last Audited:** 2026-07-23 (WRONG WAY self-HUD, presentation-only; locally browser-verified, founder sign-off pending): the existing self render frame compares normalized rendered predicted velocity with the current track tangent recovered allocation-free from the already-sampled banked-datum normal. **Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp portal founder placement; local implementation pending founder sign-off.**) The portal moved from `(-600, 250)` to the founder-chosen town-center plaza midpoint `(-547, -120)`, exactly between the bazaar east edge and the town-directory sign on the shop/sign row. `KELP_FOREST_PORTAL_WORLD_CENTER` is now founder-authoritative and boot-validated at `>=200 wu` footprint clearance from every structure plus `>=250 wu` point distance from the sign and spawn, while the former 350-wu origin-nearest derivation remains an advisory suggester only. **Last Audited:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp portal close-range walk-through repair; local implementation pending founder browser sign-off.**) The client-only solid `kelp-forest-portal` AABB remains removed, so the arch at the live `KELP_FOREST_PORTAL_WORLD_CENTER` (`-547, -120`) is physically passable. The walk-through entry gate is an allocation-free segment crossing of the portal Z plane, with X interpolated at the crossing and bounded by the 170-wu arch half-width, so diagonal edge misses and single-frame tunnelling are handled correctly. A fresh-mount seed suppresses the first-frame crossing test, every movement path re-seeds the segment origin from its authoritative frame-start position before applying movement so an external spawn or teleport cannot synthesize a crossing, and the transition trigger rejects in-flight, active, or pending duplicates. The former 120-wu arm-hysteresis latch was removed because a live walk-through showed that it swallowed legitimate crossings whose final approach began within 120 wu of the plane; it also did not protect the +240-wu return spawn, which was already beyond its arm distance. Prompt proximity remains deliberately separate from automatic entry because that return spawn is inside the 360-wu prompt radius. Both player render paths, possessed-NPC mode, and the 2D Pixi fallback use the crossing; desktop E and the tappable HUD remain shortcuts. The server route, agent action/executor/manual, protocol version, portal placement, realm, and northeast grove are unchanged.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-22 (**Kelp realm Meshy coral assets; local implementation pending founder visual sign-off.**) Founder sign-off on the reef-garden round came with one ask: real coral assets instead of procedural cone/sphere blobs. Four Meshy text-to-3D preview meshes (staghorn, fan, brain, tube cluster — `apps/web/public/models/kelp-corals/*.glb?v=1`, ~1,200 tris each, geometry-only preview mode) now provide the shapes; each deterministic wall-base placement stamps one template with per-instance rotation/scale (0.85–1.35×), a `CORAL_PALETTE` vertex-color tint, and a 105-wu normalized footprint (`normalizeKelpCoralTemplate` strips UVs, grounds the base at y=0). All stamps still merge into the SAME single lit vertex-colored decor draw call (`buildKelpCoralGardens` → `CoralGardens` component, Suspense-loaded via `useGLTFWithKTX2`, deferred-dispose lifecycle). Placement generator, seeds, wall-side verification, and exclusion zones are unchanged from the reef-garden round. Draw ledger unchanged (environment 17, worst 31 of ≤32). Per the Meshy-first asset rule these are generated assets, not hand-modeled or Mixamo-derived.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 (**Kelp realm reef-garden design round; local implementation pending founder visual sign-off.**) Founder round-2 feedback: camera still awkward, far too much fog, monochrome dark green. Fixes: (1) CAMERA — the wall-occlusion slab test is now HEIGHT-AWARE (a sightline crossing a wall above `KELP_REALM_WALL_HEIGHT_WU + 24` never clamps, because the 470-wu eye genuinely sees over the 420-wu canopy; the old 2D test clamped in every corridor), `CAM_BEHIND` 660→560 (below the 600-wu corridor width so straight corridors cannot force-clamp), and a 220-wu `CAM_MIN_DISTANCE` floor so the eye can never collapse onto the avatar's head (founder repro). (2) FOG — 1100/3200 (from 650/1750) and color lifted `0x0d4552`→`0x14586a`; corridors now read as long vistas. (3) COLOR — coral gardens replace the faint corridor accents in the SAME single decor draw call: branching corals, fan corals, and anemone tufts in `CORAL_PALETTE` pinks/oranges/magentas/golds/violets, planted only against sides verified to be REAL walls (`isKelpRealmCorridorCell` neighbor check — first iteration floated corals in open junctions), lit `MeshStandardMaterial` vertex colors; kelp roots brightened to mid-tone (`0x17705c/0x1a7a60/0x136b74`), floor to 0.18/0.36/0.36, ambient 1.05 `0xaee6dd`, rays 0.14. (4) SUNLIT DOME — camera-following 3,800-wu `SphereGeometry` dome using the proven LandingScene GradientSky recipe (CanvasTexture vertical gradient `#a5efe4→#4fb3c7→#2b7f92`, BackSide, fog:false, depthWrite:false, renderOrder −2; XZ glued to the camera in the existing environment useFrame — zero allocation); a world-sized 9,200-wu dome was far-plane clipped and never rendered. Distant blade canopy now silhouettes against the bright sky as a natural kelp horizon. Draw ledger: environment 16→17 (dome), worst total 31 of ≤32 (`KELP_REALM_SCENE_BUDGET` + scene test updated). Verified by live keyboard drive on the WebGL lane in dev; prod-bundle + staging verification follow this entry.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-21 (**Kelp realm lagoon round — prod-void fix + wide corridors + 3/4 chase camera + serene palette; local implementation pending founder visual sign-off.**) Root cause of the production "empty void" end-user report: the WebGPU backend can initialize successfully and then fail EVERY frame submit with `GPUValidationError: buffer used in submit while destroyed` (reproduced live against the prod bundle on a SwiftShader-class adapter) — no exception surfaces, so no fallback fired and the user saw only the page background plus HUD forever. Fixes: (1) all realm GPU resources (kelp/discovery/environment geometries + materials) are now created inside effects with state, never bare `useMemo`, so any cleanup+setup cycle (StrictMode dev remount, prod remount) recreates instead of leaving live meshes referencing disposed buffers; (2) a WebGPU health watchdog listens to the device `uncapturederror` stream after init — at 8 sustained errors it flags `kelp-realm-webgpu-unhealthy` in sessionStorage and reloads into the proven force-WebGL lane; (3) renderer creation is serialized through a per-canvas in-flight promise (R3F can double-invoke the async gl factory; both invocations now receive the same renderer and only the winner touches the failure store), binds ONLY the R3F canvas (never a clone — canvas swapping is what orphaned the DOM canvas), and on WebGPU init failure/hang makes ONE explicit force-WebGL second attempt on the SAME canvas; disposal of scene GPU resources is deferred two animation frames past effect cleanup so StrictMode effect replays can never destroy buffers a still-committed mesh submits; (4) every silent failure surface got UI: an animated "Entering the Kelp Forest…" loader replaces the flat dynamic-import fallback, a chunk-failure panel offers Reload, and a both-backends-failed overlay explains and offers Back to the Reef (renderer-status store `kelp-realm-renderer-status.ts`, cleared only on successful init so retry loops can't wipe the overlay). Geometry/camera: cell scale 300→600 wu (founder-directed DOUBLE of the original; footprint 6,300→12,600 wu), realm walls 500→420 wu, blade variants 320–420 wu, corridor root setback 60→96 wu (visible corridor floor 143.6→515.6 wu under opposing max sway); non-humanoid GLB avatars (lobster class) now fit by min(height-fit, 150-wu max-footprint-fit) so a low, long body can never be blown up to corridor width by height-only normalization (founder repro: screen-filling lobster). The chase eye drops from 610 to 470 wu above / 660 behind with a 150-wu look-ahead along camera forward, and the wall-occlusion clamp is now a damped scalar (tighten k=8, relax k=3) applied only to the pre-lerp target — the post-lerp hard re-clamp (the "snappy" jump) is removed. Palette: fog `0x031b20`→`0x0d4552` at 650/1,750 wu, ambient 0.95 `0x9fdcd4`, directional 1.4 `0xecfff6`, kelp roots `0x0b4f42/0x0d5a48/0x0a4a56` → tips `0x3fd9a4/0x5cebbb/0x3fd4cd`, floor multipliers 0.14/0.30/0.30, god rays opacity 0.10; page/loader backgrounds match `#0d4552`. Draw-call ledger unchanged (≤30 of ≤32). Verified rendering on BOTH the forced-WebGL and native-WebGPU lanes in dev; prod-bundle verification in progress. No layout, beacon, spore, token, route, protocol, portal, SKU, or partner behavior changed.

**Prior Audit:** 2026-07-20 (**Kelp realm serenity round; local implementation pending founder visual sign-off.**) The authored 21x21 layout is unchanged while its derived cell scale grows from 200 to 300 wu (4,200 to 6,300 wu footprint) and realm-only kelp walls drop from 800 to 500 wu. Three clustered, color-graded ribbon variants now span 390-500 wu with a bounded 100-wu tip envelope, clearly readable 3.6-4.4 second primary sway, and 1.15-1.55 second micro-ripple through identical native-WebGPU, force-WebGL node, and classic-WebGL bridge math. The chase eye sits 610 wu above the player before pitch (575-wu minimum) and uses an allocation-free player-to-camera slab test against `KELP_REALM_WALL_AABBS`, pulling before the first occluder and re-clamping after smoothing. The center reuses the floor, ray, pedestal, pearl, and mote draws, then adds one merged rotating-ring draw; all sparse corridor accents merge into one further draw and stay clear of beacon/dead-end/spore vignettes. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-16 (Reef Race chase-camera smoothness + founder board sizing): founder playtest isolated the residual moving-jitter to the SCREEN, not the kart ("other agents are smooth if I stay still"). Two live-verified camera defects: (1) ORDERING — ChaseCamera mounted before ReefRacePlayer at equal useFrame priority, so the camera read `selfPoseBus` one frame STALE every frame; the player now publishes at priority -2 and the camera reads at -1 (both negative so R3F retains automatic render; SurfBloom keeps +1). (2) HARD-SNAPPED AIM — the camera eye was lerped but `cam.lookAt()` snapped per frame to the raw predicted pose + raw `elevationAtXZ` sample, converting 30Hz prediction/reconciliation/elevation micro-noise into whole-background rotation. The look target is now a persisted vector eased with frame-rate-independent exponential damping (`1-exp(-k·dt)`, look k=12 ≈ 83ms time constant, eye k=CAMERA_LERP), seeded by hard snap on pregame/staged so no ease sweeps through geometry; elevation reaches the camera only through the damped targets (same single 'cam'-key sample per frame). RULE: never hard-lookAt a predicted pose — damp orientation and order consumers after the pose publisher. SIZING (founder-directed): `SURFBOARD_UNIFORM_SCALE` 2.5→3.4 (board ≈135wu; VRM 80wu and creature targets unchanged this round — founder judges proportion visually); `SURFBOARD_DECK_TOP_LOCAL_Y` stays symbolically derived from the same constant so riders remain deck-planted; shared `REEF_START_GRID_ROW_SPACING_WU` 120→160 (server+client+HUD single source) keeps ~25wu clearance between rows of the bigger boards, spline-sim grid test literal re-pinned 120→160. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-15 (Founder reef-race bug round — rider mount/sizing + rigid deck-plant, `ReefRacePlayer.tsx`): **(B) Board vs rider proportion + deck grounding.** Founder playtest ("board is TINY vs avatar … avatar separated/hovering above the board … feet should be planted on the board"). Measured LIVE at the `/preview` harness (board-rider-measure): surfboard length = **99.43wu** (surfboard_1.glb longest axis 1.989 local × SURFBOARD_UNIFORM_SCALE 2.5 × KART_SCALE 20), but VRM riders were fitted to **245.63wu** — the humanoid was **2.47× the board length** (board/riderHeight ratio 0.405). Retargeted `REEF_VRM_RIDER_TARGET_HEIGHT_WU` 245.63 → **80wu** so board/rider = **1.24** (a realistic surfer/longboard proportion). GLB creature riders (lobster 22wu, ratio 4.52) are UNCHANGED — the founder liked their size; the board is deliberately NOT grown (that would shrink the well-liked creatures relative to it). **(B+E) Rigid deck-plant, bob removed.** `riderMountRef` was at local Y=1.2 (24wu) while the deck top is only 0.235 local (4.7wu) — every rider floated **12–22wu above the deck** (measured), and a per-frame independent bob (`riderMount.position.y = 1.2 + sin·0.04`) added a VRM-ONLY Y shimmer the board never shared (the "self-jitter for the VRM specifically" the founder reported — the group/board pose was already smooth via the fix-your-timestep render interp). The bob is REMOVED (`_bobTime`/`BOB_AMP_LOCAL`/`BOB_FREQ_HZ` deleted); `riderMountRef` is now a STATIC deck-top plane (`SURFBOARD_DECK_TOP_LOCAL_Y` = thinnest·2.5/2 = 0.235 local for v2, `GLIDER_HEIGHT/2` for v1), so the per-rider grounding already applied at mount (VRM `computeVRMAvatarFit` offsetY / GLB bbox-min → mount origin) plants feet/body ON the deck. Measured after: feet-gap milady +22→+0.21wu, hermes +12→−0.84wu, lobster +19.4→~0wu. Board+rider now move as one rigid transform. tsc web=11 (baseline, unchanged), all 241 reef + 19 classifier tests green. Needs founder eyes on the live VRM rider. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (Founder bug batch — self-kart render smoothness, pregame camera, VRM dispose lifecycle): **(1) Self-kart render interpolation** (`ReefRacePlayer.tsx`): the v2 self kart's fixed 30Hz prediction loop previously rendered `pred` directly, so the kart advanced ~21wu on tick frames and held still on non-tick frames — constant visible self-jitter on any display >30Hz (remote karts were smooth via their 100–220ms interp path; the signed-off preview sandbox integrated per-frame). Now renders `lerp(prevTick, pred, accum/TICK_DT)` (classic fix-your-timestep, ≤33ms visual latency); reconciliation rebases shift BOTH anchors so corrections never kink the blend; `selfPoseBus` publishes the BLENDED pose so chase camera + body stay on one timebase. Dev probe `window.__REEF_RENDER_POSE` exposes the rendered pose for harness smoothness measurement. **(2) Pregame start-line camera** (`ReefRaceScene.tsx ChaseCamera`): lobby/countdown has no sim room → no entities, and the camera used to idle at the Canvas default staring at world-origin geometry until the first snapshot. It now snaps to the exact chase-cam pose of the t=0 spawn (`clientSpline.centerlineAt(0)` + tangent heading + elevation datum) so the player sees the river start through the countdown and the live transition is seamless. **(3) VRM instance dispose lifecycle** (`vrm-loader.ts` + all 10 `useVRMInstance` consumers): `disposeVRMInstance` deep-disposed synchronously with no refcount, so React StrictMode's simulated unmount (dev default) destroyed the GPU buffers of still-mounted `<primitive>` scenes → VRM NPCs rendered as invisible bodies under floating name tags with 105× "drawElements: no buffer is bound to enabled attribute" + WebGPU "buffer used in submit while destroyed". Disposal is now DEFERRED (500ms grace) and consumers call `retainVRMInstance()` in the SAME effect's setup (render-time cancel is insufficient — StrictMode re-invokes effects without re-rendering). Real unmounts still dispose. **(4) Animator double-patch false positive** (`vrm-character-animator.ts`): dispose restores a BOUND copy of `skeleton.update`, never identity-equal to `THREE.Skeleton.prototype.update`, so every legitimate animator reconstruction warned "already patched" (~40-86/load); detection now uses a static WeakSet of actively-owned skeletons. **(5) Keyboard guards**: all `e.key.toLowerCase()` handlers (World3DCanvas, npc-controller, player-avatar, use-keyboard, cove-interior) guard `e.key ?? ''` — Chrome autofill fires keydown/keyup with `key === undefined`, which crashed WASD handlers on every password-manager login. Verified headless: drawElements 0 (was 105), double-patch 0 (was 42-86), key crashes 0; smoothness probe zero-delta frames 3.6% while moving, recon 31/31 matched. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (Reef Race KTX2 staging reconciliation): the route-isolated race Canvas now initializes `KTX2LoaderSetup` before scene content, and the rider router loads staging's canonical `*-ktx.glb` creature models through `useGLTFWithKTX2`. Module-scope creature warming uses `preloadKTX2Bytes` so parsing waits for renderer capability detection. This closes the direct-activity-load failure where `MODEL_REGISTRY` supplied `KHR_texture_basisu` assets to plain `useGLTF`, while retaining the uncompressed surfboard's existing loader. No asset bytes or stable URLs changed; service-worker v9 already covers `/models/**`. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (Reef Race spline query performance): each immutable arclength-LUT entry now caches its exact centerline X/Z sample once during spline construction. `closestPointOnSpline()` scans those cached scalars instead of re-evaluating Catmull-Rom 1,001 times per query; Newton refinement and returned geometry are unchanged. The existing open/closed numerical suite remains the semantics gate and both 1,000-query performance assertions remain <50ms without relaxing thresholds. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-13 (Reef Race speed pass): founder playtest raised the shared base cap **500→650 wu/s** (+30%) and acceleration **2000→2600 wu/s²** (same 0.25s-to-cap feel). Server authority, production client prediction, and the preview tuner's canonical Reset defaults move together. Proportional mechanics retain their feel: boost-pad kick 160→208wu/s and bot ribbon lookahead 150→195wu; derived drift/mini-turbo/whirlpool thresholds follow `REEF_MAX_SPEED`. The 1.85× legitimate boost ceiling is now 1202.5wu/s and the 2.1× anti-cheat ceiling is 1365wu/s. The legacy ellipse minimum-lap detector scales 15000→11500ms so a legitimate ~13.9s base-cap lap is not rejected. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-13 (Reef Race self reconciliation): activity snapshots now carry one coherent `serverTimeMs` for init/delta/keyframe; the client maps it onto `performance.now()` using the existing pong midpoint clock-offset EWMA, compares authority with a fixed preallocated 30Hz predicted-pose ring at the matching time, and applies that error to the current pose. The >250wu teleport snap remains the raw authority-vs-current gate. Untimed/out-of-ring compatibility uses bounded dead reckoning. Remote racers retain arrival-time interpolation, now with observed-cadence/jitter adaptive delay, an 8-slot preallocated ring, duplicate-time coalescing, and ≤80ms bounded underrun extrapolation. Dev builds expose scalar diagnostics at `window.__REEF_RECON_STATS`. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-13 (Reef Race v2 port onto current staging): restored the route-isolated surf-racing scene, premium spline water/canyon renderer, self prediction, pickups/boost visuals, rider routing, preview/tuner surfaces, and reef entity fields from `recover/reef-laptop-2026-07-10` without replacing staging's hosted-agent/autonomy renderer. The existing skateboarding GLB/FBX assets were byte-identical, `agent-model-registry.ts` already exposed the required `animatorId`/`getAnimatorIdByPath`, and service-worker v9 already caches `/avatars/**`; those staging files therefore remain canonical and no asset URL/cache-version mutation was needed. See §10b.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-15 (autonomy round 2 P3c / RC6): both `VRMNpcMesh` and `GLBNpcMesh` keep the existing render-one-tick-behind entity-interpolation target, then apply frame-rate-independent exponential damping (`k=10`) from a persisted rendered XZ toward that confirmed target. This removes the visible hold at interpolation `alpha=1` between ~5 Hz snapshots without prediction, dead reckoning, or per-frame allocation. The `ts===0` client-authored/demo sentinel remains raw. `FPSFollowCamera` previously re-read unsmoothed store `x/y` rather than the rendered mesh, so its Autonomous branch now reconstructs the identical confirmed interpolation target and damping before the existing orbit-target follow; it resets on body/mode loss. See §6a, §6z stage 4, and §6f rule 5.
**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (reversed-depth label-cull regression fix): behind-camera culling for the DOM label overlay (`world-labels-overlay.tsx`) and the activities HUD helper (`world-to-screen.ts`) now tests VIEW-space z (≥ 0 = at/behind the camera plane) instead of projected NDC z. Under the r185 `reversedDepthBuffer` renderer, NDC z semantics invert (near→1, far→0) and a behind-camera point projects to z ≈ −0.002, so the old `ndc.z > 1` cull never fired — building labels rendered at mirrored screen coordinates (founder-reported on staging). The overlay's useFrame also refreshes `camera.matrixWorldInverse` once per frame before the label pass, because R3F runs useFrame subscribers before `gl.render()` and earlier subscribers mutate the camera the same frame. RULE for future overlay/HUD work: never cull or classify by projected NDC z — use view-space z; it is correct under standard Z, reversed Z, and orthographic projection.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (P1b texture-VRAM reduction): the KTX2 asset pipeline now covers the 11 resident character GLBs and compresses non-color material slots as well as color slots, while the per-instance VRM loader shares canonical textures across parses of the same VRM path with refcounted, shared-aware disposal. See §9.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (Three r185 reversed-depth adoption): the main-world `WebGPURenderer` now enables constructor-time `reversedDepthBuffer` on both its WebGPU and force-WebGL paths to improve far-plane precision across the 1–11500 camera range. Three's WebGL2 backend gracefully warns and keeps standard depth when `EXT_clip_control` is missing. `?meshlets=1` explicitly stays standard-Z because the experimental rasterizer packs, compares, reconstructs, and writes conventional NDC depth. No camera/fog/shadow/warmup values changed. Drift audit: §5g and §9c.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-14 (`World3DCanvas.tsx` first-load warmup gate + progress-aware watchdog): Canvas still initializes with `frameloop="always"` so the async R3F renderer factory runs, then `onCreated` pauses the live R3F store at runtime while the loading overlay is up. The Canvas prop is React-state-backed and changes with the store because R3F `root.configure()` re-applies the prop on every Canvas re-render; the prior store-only pause was silently reverted and leaked a measured 6.4–6.8s mid-warmup R3F render. Unified `<WorldWarmup />` replaces the racing `PreCompilePipelines` + `StaggeredTextureUpload` helpers with an ordered LoadingManager/commit barrier → stable two-zero-scan compressed-texture pre-upload → awaited `compileAsync` → one warm render → resume sequence. The original fixed 10s-from-canvas fuse was replaced after a runtime probe showed it resumed mid-download and caused a 9.9s first-render task: a 2s watchdog now fails open only after more than 10s with no loader/upload/scan/compile progress, while a separate 40s absolute ceiling stays below SeaLoadingScreen's 45s dismissal limit. Both watchdog timers clear on resume/dispose; `forceFirstPaintSizeSync` starts only after resume. Normal completion logs barrier/scans/texture-count/compile/warm-render phase timings. After readiness, a bounded 2s-cadence/60s gentle scan uploads late NPC/VRM textures. Upload counters remain monotonic across rescans; steady-state render behavior and per-frame allocation budget are unchanged. Drift audit: §5d, §5f, and §9b name the unified gate and its prop/store synchronization, pacing, ordering, and watchdog contract.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-08 (§B.1b — Autonomous-mode double-body render fix, `feat/agent-autonomy-parity` worktree `cv-autonomy-parity`): entering Autonomous previously mounted BOTH the locally-driven `PlayerAvatar` (World3DCanvas.tsx render gate was `controlMode === 'player' || controlMode === 'autonomous'`) AND the server-streamed `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` agent body that `agentAutonomyDriver`/`registerAgentBot` (npc-simulation.ts) enrolls into the SAME `npcs[]` entity-interpolation roster every other agent bot uses — two visible copies of the user's character. Fix: `stores/game.ts` gained `autonomousBodyId: string | null`, populated from the `bodyId` field `POST /api/world/autonomy {active:true}` already returns on success (world.ts, unchanged) and cleared to `null` the instant control leaves Autonomous by ANY exit path (`setControlMode`, `setAgentConnection`, `setAgentPaired`, `setHasAgent`, `resetStore` — all via the shared `leaveAutonomousServerCleanup` helper plus per-method belt-and-suspenders clears). `World3DCanvas.tsx`: (1) the `PlayerAvatar` + `ClickToMove` render gates are now `controlMode === 'player'` ONLY — Autonomous shows zero local-avatar geometry; (2) `FPSFollowCamera` gained an `autonomous` branch that looks up the streamed body by `autonomousBodyId` in `useNpcStore` (same lookup-by-id pattern as the existing `npc`-possession branch) instead of reading the now-frozen `avatarPositionRef`, holding camera position (no-op that frame) until the id is confirmed/streamed in — never snapping to the map origin; (3) `MinimapPositionTracker` gained the identical branch so the minimap blip also tracks the streamed body instead of freezing; (4) `setControlMode` now also clears `nearLocation`/`nearCharacter` on ENTRY to Autonomous (same treatment as entering `explore`) — without this, a proximity prompt captured the instant before the toggle would freeze non-null forever (PlayerAvatar's per-frame proximity check is what normally keeps it current, and PlayerAvatar is unmounted in this mode), letting the spectating human fire a stale "press E to enter" against a building the agent has long since left. Diagnostic: `World3DCanvas.tsx`'s existing `window.__R3F` DEBUG PROBE (PreCompilePipelines) gained a sibling `window.__CV_STORES__ = { useGameStore, useNpcStore }` — same devtools-diagnostic convention, lets CDP/puppeteer force `controlMode`/`autonomousBodyId` and inject a synthetic streamed npc entry to verify hide/show/camera logic without a live backend/agent stack. Browser-verified headless (puppeteer-core + local Chrome, `bun run build && bun run start` prod bundle, fetch stubbed only for `/api/world/autonomy`): player mode → `perf:player-avatar` scene group present, camera close-follow; autonomous mode → group ABSENT, camera retargets to the synthetic body's live position (tracked its NPC-wander drift correctly, proving live re-lookup not a frozen snapshot), WorldLabelsOverlay renders its name label like any other npc; flip back to player → group reappears same tick, `autonomousBodyId` null, no ghost. `tsc --noEmit` unchanged at 11 pre-existing errors (0 new) both before and after. Codex-reviewed (see PR/commit notes) per Rule E3.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-07-07 (P1 texture-memory fix: world-geometry GLB baseColor/emissive textures converted to new sibling `*-ktx.glb` files with ETC1S/KHR_texture_basisu, leaving normal/metallicRoughness/occlusion slots uncompressed; source GLBs stay on disk as rollback. Runtime loaders that consume converted GLBs now attach `KTX2Loader` through `KTX2LoaderSetup`/`useGLTFWithKTX2` or explicit manual `GLTFLoader.setKTX2Loader`, and module-scope boot preloads for converted assets fetch bytes only until a Canvas renderer can detect support. `sw.js` cache version bumped to v9 for the new URLs.)

> **Strict rule:** every code change that touches world dimensions, camera,
> lighting, fog, GPU constraints, animation systems, terrain shaders,
> asset-load pipeline, or activity-room rendering MUST update this doc in the
> same diff. Reverse holds. Mismatch is a bug.
>
> Companions:
> - **`WorldContent.md`** — *what* is in the scene (buildings, NPCs, props). Use that doc when the question is "what renders here".
> - **`ARCHITECTURE.md`** — backend services, routes, schema. Activity sim services live there (§4).
> - **`GameFeatures.md`** — gameplay.
> - **This doc** — *how* the 3D scene is wired: coordinates, camera, lights, GPU budget, animation, asset pipeline.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-20 (fog/far PULLBACK — fixes "much more pixelly" on Iris-Xe). Founder reported the world got much more pixelly after a recent push. Root cause traced: NOT a DPR/antialias change (those are the long-standing Iris-Xe `LOW_END_DPR_RANGE=[0.55,0.7]` + `antialias:false`, unchanged since May) and NOT avatar decimation — it was the **2026-06-15 Phase-0-land world expansion** (grid 360→576, `MAP_WIDTH` 11520→18432wu) which pushed fog OUT (`[6500,13500]`) and camera.far OUT (10000→14000) to fit the bigger world. That re-exposed a large sprawl of distant low-resolution geometry (far building ring + outer land parcels at up to ~12309wu from center) that, at the low Iris-Xe DPR, reads as pixelly/aliased. **Fix (`World3DCanvas.tsx`):** pulled fog back to `[FOG_COLOR, 5000, 10500]` and `camera.far` 14000→11500 (fog.far ≤ camera.far invariant kept; geometry fully fades to fog by 10500 BEFORE the 11500 frustum cull, so no hard pop). Matches the founder's documented 2026-05-22 direction ("target the fog more, i don't care about it much"). Building ring (R=130=4160wu, ≤8320wu across) stays clearly visible; everything past ~10500wu is hidden in fog and culled past 11500 (reclaims fill/geometry cost = FPS gain, "free" — no resolution change). **Trade-off (needs founder eyes, Rule E4):** outer land parcels viewed from spawn now fog/cull out and reappear as you walk toward them; if the founder wants more of the city visible at once, push fog/far back out (e.g. `[5500,12500]` / far 13000). DPR/FXAA sharpness bump is the SEPARATE next lever (costs FPS) — deferred pending this free pass. Frontend-only, no schema. NOT yet verified in-browser (chrome-devtools MCP disconnected).

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-18 (S4 — labels now occlude behind the local player). The DOM-label occluder (`world-labels-overlay.tsx` `_checkOcclusion`, 2 Hz staggered) previously raycast ONLY building meshes (`userData.isOccluder`); avatars were never occluders, so an NPC name/chat label BEHIND the player drew over the player's body. Added an analytic ray-sphere test of the local player avatar (torso sphere centre `(avatarPositionRef−HALF, −2+135+jumpAltitude)`, R=80) on the camera→NPC-**BODY** ray — NOT the high label point (offset +320 above the head; a torso sphere misses a ray aimed there), while buildings still test the label point. Mode-gated to player/npc/autonomous (explore has no body); the possessed-player "You" label + self speech bubble carry `skipLocalAvatarOcclusion` so the player's own body can't hide its own label. Player-only (all-avatar-mutual deferred — needs a dedicated occluder registry). Pure scalar math at the existing 2 Hz cadence (no alloc/raycast → S8-safe). Files: `world-labels-overlay.tsx`, `arena-npcs.tsx`, `npc-speech-bubbles.tsx`. Codex co-reviewed (Rule E3): caught that a torso sphere on the label ray was a no-op → switched to the body ray.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-18 (S7 — held input no longer sticks when a window steals focus). NEW `apps/web/src/lib/three/input-reset.ts` centralizes ONE set of window `blur`/`focus`/`pageshow`/`visibilitychange` listeners and fans out to a registry of reset callbacks, so every held-input vector releases on focus loss AND regain (browser skips `keyup` off-window). Wired: player WASD (`player-avatar.tsx`) + NPC WASD (`npc-controller.tsx`) — replaced their partial per-module blur resets; jump SPACE (`jump-state.ts` `resetJumpInput` — cancels a mid-charge back to grounded so focus-loss can't FIRE an unintended hop, leaves airborne physics intact); arrow-orbit + explore-mode WASD-pan cameras (`World3DCanvas.tsx`); open-world + activity mobile joysticks (`mobile-controls.tsx`, `activity-mobile-controls.tsx`); activity keyboard (`useActivityInput.ts` migrated to the shared util); Pixi 2D fallback (`use-keyboard.ts`). Root cause: the April WASD blur-reset covered only player/NPC vectors — explore-pan, arrow-orbit and jump had none, so a popup over the game left the camera/avatar "stuck moving." Frontend-only, no schema.

First representative section: north-side starter ring (parcels 0-5), east-side founder ring (parcels 2-4), south-side c-ring (parcels 14-17). Sea-themed merged props (coral-reef1/2/3.glb, kelp.glb, building-barrel.glb, building-lantern.glb, building-anchor.glb, building-shell.glb) + flat path ribbons connecting parcels. All geometry merged by material UUID (pattern mirrors MergedDecorationsInner in arena-terrain.tsx): ~10-14 added draw calls total for ALL props, plus 1 draw call for all path ribbons (shared MeshStandardMaterial). Model paths overlap with DeferredTerrainPreloads — zero additional network cost. Constraints: no InstancedMesh, no ShaderMaterial/NodeMaterial, no drei Text/Billboard, no per-frame allocs, matrixAutoUpdate=false + computeBoundingBox on every merged mesh. Fog near=5000/far=10500 naturally distance-culls c-ring from spawn. Mounted in World3DCanvas.tsx as `perf:land-ring-decorations` group between LandShowroom and the buildings group. Does NOT touch parcel geometry or economy. Scale: expand fromIdx/toIdx ranges in generateRingSectionProps() to cover full rings once founder approves this section. See §13 below.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-18 (jump BUG 1 squat — v3 foot-grounding REVERTED; interim = stand-during-charge; real fix = re-bake clip). **BUG 1 (midair squat) — STILL OPEN, runtime approaches abandoned.** The `squat` clip is ROTATION-ONLY (raw hips.position Y flat constant 104.226, zero descent; knees barely bend, headless-measured foot rise ~2.3 wu). THREE runtime fixes all failed: (a) `getSquatGroundLift` preserve-descent → no-op (descent=0); (b) `crouchDepth` translate + replant → cancels (rigid-body translate can't synthesize a crouch); (c) **v3 foot-grounding** (`group.y -= (getFootWorldYMin() − floor)`) → **live render OSCILLATED ~per-frame between standing and half-sunk** (violent flicker). Codex review (Rule E3, thread 019ed912): `group.updateMatrixWorld(true)` DOES refresh the normalized hierarchy (it's under `vrm.scene` under the group; `getWorldPosition` self-updates) — so my "stale read" hypothesis was wrong; the real faults are (1) `getFootWorldYMin` reads the NORMALIZED control rig, not the RAW skinned skeleton the mesh uses, and (2) the animator monkey-patches `Skeleton.update` to a manual flush, so a group transform AFTER that flush leaves rendered skinning one phase out → oscillation. **Decision: abandon runtime grounding** (it can only recover the clip's ~2.3 wu — can't make "pelvis lowers, feet planted"; needs authored data or IK). **Interim (this commit):** squat-charge plays `'idle'` (avatar stands, movement still halted by `chargeMode`, no glitch) in `player-avatar.tsx` + `arena-npcs.tsx`; grounding blocks removed. **Real fix (pending, asset):** re-bake the squat clip (Mixamo squat WITHOUT in-place → real hips-Y descent + knee flexion), rebundle `_emotes.glb` bumping `EMOTE_BUNDLE_VERSION` (vrm-character-animator.ts) + `EMOTE_BUNDLE` (asset-preload-manifest.ts); the retargeter preserves hips-Y so NO runtime grounding is needed; keep `squat` OUT of `IN_PLACE_CLIPS`. `getFootWorldYMin()` left in place (unused) for a possible future IK path. **BUG 2 (foot-slide) — DONE + LIVE:** `jumpState.chargeMode` (`'squat'|'run'|'none'`) set on rising edge in both files; walk/idle → halt WASD; run → keep running. §7a updated.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-17 (Land structures STAGE 2 — in-world placed-structure render scaffold). New `apps/web/src/lib/three/land-structures.tsx` draws a player's placed home/shop on its parcel — TODAY as a clean low-poly primitive fallback, with the real GLBs swapping in AFTER the founder picks a Stage-1 style. **Store extension** (`apps/web/src/stores/land.ts`): additive `structures: Map<parcelId, PlacedStructure>` (`{parcelId, catalogKey, structureType, level}`) + `setStructures(list)` (FULL replace of the visible owner's set) + `getStructure(map, id)` helper; existing `parcels`/`setParcels`/`release`/`getParcelStatus` untouched. **catalogKey → GLB map:** EXHAUSTIVE over the 12 SKUs via a guaranteed default branch — every `home-*` key → one `home.glb`, every `shop-*` key → one `shop.glb` of the DEFAULT style (`/models/land-structures/coastal-cottage/{home,shop}.glb`, marked `// TODO-SWAP` — Stage-1 may not have shipped these yet). **Primitive fallback** (`PrimitiveStructure`): box body + 4-sided cone (pyramid) roof + door slab, `MeshStandardMaterial` (NO ShaderMaterial), tinted by structureType (warm cottage tones for home, cool commercial for shop). EVERY placed structure renders SOMETHING immediately even with no GLB present — each GLB-backed structure is wrapped in BOTH a `<Suspense fallback={<PrimitiveStructure/>}>` (covers the PENDING load) AND a class `GLBErrorBoundary` (covers the drei-`useGLTF` REJECTION on a 404 — Suspense alone does NOT catch a load rejection, only the pending promise), so a missing/failed GLB always shows the primitive, never a hole or a crash. **Sizing:** structure normalized so its widest XZ dim = `parcel.size × 0.62` at level 1 (so the for-sale frame still reads around it; parcel.size = 192wu founder / 224wu others → level-1 footprint ≈ 119/139wu), GLBs via max(X,Y,Z) bbox normalization + grounded (subtract `bbox.min.y × scale` so feet sit on `FLOOR_Y = -2`). **level → scale ramp:** `levelScale(level) = 0.78 + (clamp(level,1,5)−1) × 0.1175` → L1=0.78, L2=0.8975, L3=1.015, L4=1.1325, L5=1.25. **Far-cull:** per-structure `useFrame` toggles `.visible` by SQUARED camera→parcel distance; GENEROUS `CULL_DIST = 14000wu` (outer parcels reach ~12309wu corner distance from origin; building GLBs are never culled, so 14000 is consistent + never vanishes an on-screen structure — avoids the too-tight-cull permanent-hidden bug); module-scope scratch `Vector3`, ZERO per-frame allocations. **Added draw budget:** one owner has ≤ `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR` (5) parcels, so worst case ≤5 VISIBLE structures (primitive ≈3 draws each, low-poly GLB small) — tiny. **Self-hydration:** `StructureHydrator` best-effort `api.getMyLand()` on mount → `setStructures(...)` so the signed-in OWNER sees their own placed buildings in-world WITHOUT any modal edit; try/catch, never throws, never blocks, swallows guest/network failures. **Mount:** `World3DCanvas.tsx`, `<Suspense fallback={null}><group name="perf:land-structures" perfChunk="land-structures"><LandStructures/></group></Suspense>` right after the `perf:land-parcels` group. **TODO:** final GLB paths swap in after the founder picks a Stage-1 style (bump `?v=N` on the new asset URLs). The ONE remaining wire NOT done: OTHER players' structures (the modal-driven `getOwnedLand(avatarId)` path) — out of this layer's scope.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-15 (perf round-3 — load-phase loosen-while-loading model). Three load-phase bottlenecks fixed under the loading spinner; steady-state (82 FPS) untouched. (A) **Second `compileAsync` after VRM batch drains.** `PreCompilePipelines` (`World3DCanvas.tsx`) now registers a `registerBulkVRMIdleCallback` (new export from `vrm-loader.ts`) that fires once the VRM parse queue goes fully idle for the first time. The callback kicks a second `gl.compileAsync(scene, camera)` so the 266 skinned-`MeshStandardMaterial` pipeline variants that load asynchronously over ~10s compile under the spinner via KHR_parallel_shader_compile instead of lazily at reveal (~7.5s main-thread self-time in the SwiftShader trace). No `gl.render()` call — blue-screen safe. (B) **Fast texture upload while loading overlay is up.** `StaggeredTextureUpload` now switches between two pacing modes based on `__W3D_READY`: *fast* (30ms budget / 32-tex cap per idle slice; 24-tex per rAF tick) while `__W3D_READY !== true`, *gentle* (6ms / 4-tex / 4-rAF) once the world is interactive. Helper `isLoadingOverlayUp()` does the check. The existing two-rAF gate, `markTextureUploadReady()` / `gl.render()` call, and `__W3D_TEXTURES_READY` / `__W3D_TEXTURE_UPLOAD_TOTAL|DONE` counter semantics are all preserved exactly. (C) **Dynamic VRM parse concurrency.** `vrm-loader.ts` constant `VRM_PARSE_CONCURRENCY = 2` replaced with `VRM_PARSE_CONCURRENCY_BULK = 6` / `VRM_PARSE_CONCURRENCY_STEADY = 2`; `getVRMParseConcurrency()` picks based on `__W3D_READY`. Priority lane (player-avatar `unshift`) and generation-guard cancellation (`VRM_LOAD_GEN`) are unchanged. (D) **`SLOW_MS` 15s → 8s** in `sea-loading-screen.tsx` — "taking longer than expected" fires after 8s instead of 15s, matching the new expected warm-load window. `TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000` unchanged.

See §6c sizing note. (2) **FIX-15 — defensive walk in-place strip.** `PER_CHARACTER_IN_PLACE_CLIPS['hermes-male']` gained `'walk'` (alongside `'run'`) so the latent broken `hermes-male/walk.glb` on-disk bake (+1.887 hips-Y/loop, dropped from the override JSON in `8fd93b32` but left on disk) stays sink-safe if ever re-wired; a nested `$comment` in `character-anim-overrides.json` documents the trap. hermes-male/Phanes intentionally use the clean GLOBAL `walk.glb` (net −0.845 hips-Y). See §6c in-place toggle note.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-12 (Codex finding #5 — remote players FREEZE after mount): the D3a fix below cured the load deadlock but NOT the steady-state freeze — remotes mounted once then never moved. Root cause: the players store MUTATED player objects in place (`updateFromSnapshot` reused the prev reference when `fieldsEqual`), so `player` identity never changed; `RemotePlayerEntry`'s `adaptPlayer` is `useMemo([player])`, which therefore never recomputed and the entry's `npcRef.current` held the snapshot position captured at MOUNT. (Wandering NPCs survive in-place mutation because the mesh consumes the store object DIRECTLY — `npcRef.current` points at the live mutated object — but remote players go through the `adaptPlayer` COPY, which froze.) **Fix:** `players.ts updateFromSnapshot` now does IMMUTABLE updates — any player whose `x/y/dirZ/activity/identity/isLocal` changed gets a NEW object (identity flips), while a perfectly still player keeps its reference. So a moved remote player: re-renders through memo → recomputes the adapter → refreshes `npcRef.current` with the latest `prevX/x/ts/tsDelta` → the mesh's entity-interp lerps `prevX→x` → VISIBLY moves. Still players cost zero reconciliation (memo bails). `setLocalSessionId` re-stamp is also immutable now for consistency. Files: `stores/players.ts`, `lib/three/remote-players.tsx` (comment-only, the memo+Suspense-outside structure is unchanged). **Prior same-day edit:** D3 remote-player render fix — two bugs: **(A) D3a — permanent zero-mesh for ALL remote players including preloaded species:** Root cause: `React.memo + Suspense-inside-memo deadlock`. `RemotePlayerEntry` was wrapped in `React.memo` and contained a `<Suspense>` boundary internally. The players store's identity-mutation pattern (`updateFromSnapshot` in `players.ts:116-127` mutates the existing object in-place when `fieldsEqual` is true and pushes the SAME reference to the new array) means the `player` prop identity NEVER changes after the first snapshot — `React.memo` bails out permanently. React's Suspense retry mechanism needs to re-render THROUGH the memo boundary to schedule a retry render for the suspended subtree inside it; the memo bailout returns the previous (suspended) render output unchanged, causing permanent `fallback={null}` — zero visible mesh — for any species regardless of whether the VRM was preloaded (milady_official_1, hermes-male, etc.). **Fix:** `<Suspense key={p.id} fallback={null}>` moved to the `RemotePlayers` map level (outside `React.memo`); `RemotePlayerEntry` now returns `<VRMNpcMesh npc={npcLike} />` directly (Suspense promise propagates up to the parent boundary). One per-player boundary also prevents player A's stalled VRM load from blocking player B's render. Additionally: `preloadVRMBytes(regEntry.path)` called eagerly inside `RemotePlayerEntry` for out-of-preload-list species (phanes, eliza-chibi, milady-chibi, hatcher_1-8); `VRM_PARSE_CONCURRENCY` raised 1→2 to halve queue wait for out-of-list species. **(B) D3b — giant body (~10x):** `computeVRMAvatarFit` (`vrm-avatar-sizing.ts`) now calls `sm.skeleton.update()` on every SkinnedMesh before `Box3.setFromObject`. Root cause: `useMemo([vrm, npc.species])` runs synchronously on first render before any animator tick — skeleton `boneMatrices` are zero → every vertex maps to near-origin → `size.y ≈ 0` → `scale = VRM_AVATAR_FALLBACK_SCALE (169)`. For a Mixamo cm-rig (native bbox ~194u) that is 169 × 194 = 32,786wu — the documented giant. `skeleton.update()` settles bind-pose matrices so the bbox reflects the actual rest-pose silhouette. [[feedback_applybonetransform_needs_skeleton_update]] race; affects all Mixamo-rigged VRMs (hermes-male, hermes-female, tekk, phanes, eliza-chibi, hatcher_1-8).

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-11 (hermes/tekk locomotion root-motion sinking fix): `PER_CHARACTER_IN_PLACE_CLIPS` in `vrm-character-animator.ts` extended with `'hermes-male': {run}`, `'hermes-female': {run}`, `'tekk': {run, walk}`. These four per-character Mixamo override bakes were fetched BEFORE the In-Place flag was forced on in `fetch-animations.ts` and carried non-cyclic hips Y drift (hermes-male run +3.40wu/loop, hermes-female +2.91, tekk run +2.34, tekk walk +1.60). The global `IN_PLACE_CLIPS` set covers the shared global `run.glb` but NOT per-character override paths (resolved via `resolveAnimPath` → character-specific GLB), so `shouldStripPosition` was missing these. Adding them to `PER_CHARACTER_IN_PLACE_CLIPS` is the minimal runtime-only fix. Global idles/walk/run and all chibi clips are clean (cyclic, net ~0). Diagnostic: `gltf-transform` hips-Y first/last track dump on meshopt-decoded GLBs.

Asset pipeline also included texture-only building recompression, bazaar stand compression, quest-bounty pavilion compression, SW cache updates, and the documented rule that building GLBs flowing through `mergeStaticMeshesByMaterial` must not be meshopt-quantized.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-06 (perf2 adaptive FPS pass): `World3DCanvas.tsx` now resolves default `/game` perf through `useAdaptiveWorldPerfFlags()` when no manual `perfFlags` are passed. Quality tiers sample RAF every 2.5s after a 5s warmup: tier 1 disables animated ground cover (`MergedSeaweed`), tier 2 disables activity/reward FX, tier 3 disables world labels, and tier 4 switches far building residents/companions plus all 12 building GLBs to shared, group-cleared cheap geometry proxies while preserving full resident GLB detail when the camera comes within 2600wu (demote again after 3200wu). At tier 4, `lod-orchestrator.tsx` also tightens moving full-detail NPC/remote-player budget to 2 nearest entities; the rest use the existing capsule proxy path, and `AdaptiveRendererDpr` clamps renderer DPR to `0.4` until quality recovers. The governor steps down when FPS <58 and restores one tier only after 3 high samples >90 FPS, so capable GPUs keep full quality while low-end/mobile devices do not bounce back into a slower tier too eagerly. `?fast=1` locks tier 4 for deterministic measurement. Manual `/perf` flags bypass the governor. `WorldPerfFlags` split fog from decorative effects via new `groundCover`, `activityFx`, `residentDetail`, and `buildingDetail` flags, so fog remains available when seaweed/FX/resident/building detail are off. Low-end DPR is now actually `[0.5,0.65]` in both Canvas and async WebGPU init (the previous code still used `[0.55,0.7]` despite the doc comment), with the tier-4 emergency clamp layered on top. `gpu-tier.ts` owns the shared synchronous low-end probe. `lod-orchestrator.tsx` keeps `FULL_CAP=14` on capable GPUs but uses `ACTIVE_FULL_CAP=8` on low-end/mobile for wandering/remote entities until tier 4; the possessed NPC remains full. `/perf` adds decorative-FX-off and far-resident-proxy tests plus separate toggles for fog, ground cover, activity FX, and resident detail. No new per-frame allocations, no new lights, no drei Text/Billboard, no InstancedMesh.

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-06-06: Multiplayer Phase 1 render fixes (FIX #4 + FIX #6 + cheap minors). **(FIX #4 -- remote-player push-out desync):** `arena-npcs.tsx` GLBNpcMesh and VRMNpcMesh entity-vs-local-player push-out blocks now guard on `!d.isRemotePlayer`. Remote players routed through these components (via `remote-players.tsx` adaptPlayer) were being pushed by each client's own avatar position -- a different vector per client -- causing per-client visual divergence and breaking the authoritative-shared-world premise. The AABB building clamp (`clampMovement2D`) is still applied for remote players (static colliders are per-client identical, so the clamp is benign). `NpcSpriteState.isRemotePlayer?: boolean` added to `stores/npc.ts` with JSDoc explaining why the push-out must be skipped. `adaptPlayer` in `remote-players.tsx` sets `isRemotePlayer: true`. **(FIX #6 -- remote players ignore server heading):** `adaptPlayer` now sets `facingAngle: player.dirZ` (previously hardcoded null). VRMNpcMesh facing block updated to add a server-authoritative override branch: when `d.facingAngle != null`, use it directly (shortest-path slerp) before falling through to the velocity-derived `atan2(vx,vz)` path. Same convention: server `dirZ = atan2(dx,dy)`, renderer `targetRot = atan2(vx,vz)`, same space, no negations (VRM facing formula locked 2026-04-25). **(Cheap minors):** `adaptPlayer` now derives `isRunning: player.activity === 'running'` (was omitted, remote players never played the run animation); `isOpenClaw: player.kind === 'agent'` was already set correctly.

Fixed: `CAM_LOOK_Y` −350→**+220** (POSITIVE) so the camera looks near-horizontal toward open water; `CAM_Y` 454→**380** to further flatten the shot. **Last edit:** 2026-06-01 (later) — Reef Race v2 **wide water-dominant overhaul** (supersedes the skinny corridor from the surf rebuild below). **Course:** `track-layout.ts` corridor widened from halfWidth 290–540 (the skinny-canal bug) to **1300 lagoon/finish · 1200 sweeping straights · ~960 chicane pinch** (corridor 2400–2600wu ≈ 54 kart-diameters — fits all 8 racers + overtaking; min corridor 960 > the 590wu 8-kart floor). 16 CPs, three WIDE sweeping bends (centerline max|x|=606 ≈ 25% of corridor so water NEVER walls), arc 28356wu, min radius 1591wu. Steering now matters by **course design, not walls**: 6 slow urchin-rock **obstacle clusters** (`buildSplineObstacles()` mirroring ramps; `resolveObstacles()` in the sim applies `hazard-slow` −40%/200ms) sit on the inside line of each bend + dead-center at the 2 chicanes, so cutting the apex is faster only if you thread them. Server `getStaticZones()` now emits the clusters as `reefStaticZones.hazards` and `activity-ws-hub` wires them into `snapshot.init` so the client `ReefRaceHazards` renders them (was hard-omitted for the spline path). The "STEERING IS MANDATORY / straight-line-exits-corridor" test (which CAUSED the skinny bug) was replaced with 3 design guards (centerline bends ≥300, straight corridor ≥2000, chicane pinch ∈[800,1200]). **Water-dominant scene:** `river-scene.tsx` land bands shrunk — `GROUND_INNER_OFFSET` 873→250, `GROUND_W` 6000→2500, `LATERAL_BAND_MIN/MAX` 180/600→120/350, `GATE_POST_HALF_W` 520→1400, `BRIDGE_W` 1100→2800, `MARKER_X_OFFSET`→1800 — water rises from ~4% to ~27–34% of the cross-section (fixes "90% land for a water game"). **Flowing water:** `water-surf.tsx` was flat because the glint used a hardcoded `(0,1,0)` normal + no flow; now computes **analytical gradient normals** from the 3-octave displacement (finite-diff eps=2) so highlights ride the crests, adds downstream Z flow, faster UV scroll (0.10/0.18), crest foam from `vDisp`, current-line streaks, and `uSunDir` redirected to −Z so specular reflects toward the chase cam; `RIBBON_SAMPLES` 64→128 to kill displacement aliasing. **Camera:** `CAMERA_OFFSET` −530→**−700** behind, Y 320→**360**, to frame the wide channel + bends. All on branch `feat/reef-overhaul-v2` (worktree off staging). 45/45 reef sim tests pass with the flag.

**Last edit:** 2026-06-01 — Reef Race v2 surf rebuild. **Visual:** the gameplay track layer (SplineTrack bed, players, pickups, checkpoints, start grid, finish gate, ribbons, hazards, apex markers) is wrapped in `<group position-y={TRACK_SURFACE_Y}>` in `ReefRaceScene.tsx` (`TRACK_SURFACE_Y = -200`, = WaterSurf `WATER_Y`) and the chase camera's target/eye/lookAt base Y shifts to the same — so the surfer rides ON the flowing water inside the rocky-cliff ravine. `RiverScene` (sky dome, water, terrain, `RockyCliffs`) stays at ABSOLUTE world Y. SplineTrack river-bed at local −2 (world −202, just under the surface); old grass-green bank walls `visible={false}` (RockyCliffs are the canyon walls). Fixes the "water disappeared, surfer on a green chunky track" report — the camera was framing the Y=0 grass plane 200wu above the water. **Physics:** velocity-steering replaced by surf-carving — heading-rate turn + lateral grip + carried momentum + coast-on-release — in the pure shared `integrateSurfStep` (`packages/shared/src/reef-race/surf-physics.ts`), run by the server per 30Hz tick AND mirrored client-side. Corridor tightened (halfWidth ~290–540, slalom ±440–460 > halfWidth) so a straight line is walled off → steering mandatory; jumps cut turn authority only (not forward speed); collisions spread over ticks. **Client feel:** self-kart client-side prediction (fixed 1/30 accumulator + per-snapshot re-baseline pos0.4/vel0.5/rot0.5, hard-snap >250wu) + chase camera follows the predicted pose via `selfPoseBus` (150ms stale fallback to server-interp) — kills the ~300ms input lag + body-vs-camera rubber-band. New `reef-race-self-bus.ts`. All client behavior gated `NEXT_PUBLIC_REEF_RACE_USE_SPLINE && isSelf`; remote karts + v1 ellipse path render byte-identically. Both server `REEF_RACE_USE_SPLINE` + client `NEXT_PUBLIC_REEF_RACE_USE_SPLINE` are ON on staging (verified on the live containers).

**Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-28 — Wandering NPC locomotion animation is speed-matched to rendered XZ translation; see §13 for tunables and phase-continuity note.

**Last edit:** 2026-05-27 — Multiplayer Phase 1 client: distance-LOD orchestrator + remote-player render path. New `lod-orchestrator.tsx` (mounted inside R3F canvas) collects wandering NPCs + remote players, frustum-tests them, sorts by camera distance², promotes the top 14 to full VRM/GLB (`VRMNpcMesh` / `GLBNpcMesh` — both now exported), and demotes the rest to a cheap `EntityProxyMesh` (one module-scope `CapsuleGeometry(25, 120, 4, 8)` + per-instance `MeshBasicMaterial` clone tinted by entity color + reused `WorldLabel` rig + zero spring bones / mixer / VRM parse). `FULL_CAP=14`, `VISIBILITY_RADIUS_WU=3500`, `HYSTERESIS_MS=500` (entities at the 14/15 boundary stay in their current tier for ≥ 500 ms before flipping — kills boundary flicker). Hysteresis state tracked per-entity in a `Map<id, {tier, lastFlippedAt}>` on a `useRef`, garbage-collected when > 64 entries. Shared tier registry in `useLodStore` (Zustand `Set<string>`); writer (orchestrator) calls `setFullSet(next)` which does a cheap size-and-membership equality check before bumping `revision`. Readers (`NpcEntry` in `arena-npcs.tsx`, `RemotePlayerEntry` in `remote-players.tsx`) subscribe per-entity via `useLodStore((s) => s.fullSet.has(id))` so a single tier flip re-renders only that entity. Building residents (`ArenaLocationNpcs`) are EXCLUDED from the candidate pool — always full. The possessed `PLAYER_NPC` is also force-full to avoid demoting the user's controlled avatar mid-play. Remote-player render path adapts `RemotePlayerState → NpcSpriteState`-shaped object so `VRMNpcMesh` / `GLBNpcMesh` are reused unchanged; per-player `sessionId` is the VRM-loader `instanceId`, so two remote players on the same Milady species get distinct `(path, instanceId)` cache entries with no cross-clobber. New `usePlayerStore` mirrors `useNpcStore`'s entity-interp pattern (`prevX/prevY/ts/tsDelta`). `use-world-stream.ts` REPLACES `use-npc-stream.ts` (game/page.tsx swap): reads `?room=CODE`, POSTs `/api/world/join` (returns `{ roomId, sessionId }`), opens SSE `/api/world/:roomId/stream`, uploads local `avatarPositionRef` + heading + activity at 5 Hz via `POST /api/world/position`, routes the unified snapshot through both NPC and player stores. Sidebar gains a Room/Invite chip (4-char roomId + copy-link button → `${origin}/game?room=CODE`). Iris Xe invariants preserved: no drei `<Text>`/`<Billboard>`, no `InstancedMesh + ShaderMaterial`, no per-frame allocs (module-scope `_camPos`, `_frustum`, `_projScreenMatrix`, `_entitySphere`, `_candidates[]` scratch in orchestrator). §5d throttle table gets the new multiplayer-LOD row. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-26 — SeaLoadingScreen dismissal re-gated on `window.__W3D_READY` (canvas first-frame AND `StaggeredTextureUpload` completion) instead of `window.__W3D` (canvas first-frame only). The first-frame-only signal was dismissing the overlay before WebGPU texture uploads finished, leaving the user staring at a blue/blank world under the UI. The `__W3D_READY` two-gate flag was added in `43a9000d` then reverted in `1ffc40a8`; this re-applies it on the loader side only — World3DCanvas already sets both `__W3D_CANVAS_READY` + `__W3D_TEXTURES_READY` and ANDs them into `__W3D_READY`. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-25 — Phase 6.4.0: blackjack table click hotspot added to cove interior. `BlackjackTableHotspot` component at `_BJ_HOTSPOT_POS = [307, 100, 0]` (dealer station right wall). Invisible `boxGeometry` 200×200×150 wu + `meshBasicMaterial visible={false}`. `onClick` → `useCoveStore.openBlackjackTable()`. Green `BankBanner` ("BLACKJACK", `#22dd88`) at Y=280wu. `BlackjackModal` mounted in `/cove/page.tsx` alongside `SlotScreenModal`. Mock route `POST /api/cove/blackjack/play-mock-hand` registered in `apps/api/src/index.ts`. Connection SKILL.md + hosted-agent skill memory deferred to Phase 6.4.2 per plan. See §10c. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-23 — Phase A LOD hierarchy added to meshlet rasterizer. `geometryToMeshletAsset()` in `nanite-rasterizer.ts` now builds up to 7 LOD levels using `MeshoptSimplifier.simplify()` (~50% tri reduction per step, error thresholds doubling: 0/0.01/0.02/0.04/0.08/0.15/0.25). All LODs share the same vertex pool (meshoptimizer preserves original vertex indices). `computeFrustum` TSL compute shader upgraded from hardcoded `uint(0)` to JS-unrolled cascading `If/ElseIf` chain (coarsest-to-finest) selecting the coarsest LOD whose projected screen-space error ≤ `pixelErrorThreshold=4.0px`. Projected error formula: `lodError × instanceScale × cotHalfFov × (screenH/2) / distToCamera`. `MeshletAsset` extended with `lodErrors: Float32Array` and `lodTriCounts: number[]`. `geometryToMeshletAssetAsync()` async wrapper added. Overlay updates: `/preview/meshlet-spike` shows LOD count + coarsest-LOD reduction pct; `/preview/meshlet-spike-all-12` shows "Coarsest LOD tris" + "LOD reduction %" header rows and per-building `×NLODs` suffix. Goal: from 7 FPS (12 buildings, sandy-treedome 1.13M tris, 0 LOD) to 60+ FPS (LOD selection selecting ~4× fewer chunks at typical 5000wu camera distance). **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-23 — Phase A meshlet spike extended to all 12 buildings. New route `/preview/meshlet-spike-all-12` (`apps/web/src/app/preview/meshlet-spike-all-12/page.tsx`). Loads all 12 building GLBs in parallel via `Promise.all` + `GLTFLoader.setMeshoptDecoder(MeshoptDecoder)`, merges each building's sub-meshes into one `BufferGeometry` (world-transform applied), calls `geometryToMeshletAsset()`, then instantiates **12 separate `NaniteRasterizer` instances** (one per building at its ring-slot position, scale=1.0). Design rationale: `NaniteRasterizer` bakes geometry data into TSL `StorageBufferAttribute` nodes at `_buildPipelines()` time; `staticInstanceData` is `vec4(posX,posY,posZ,scale)` with no per-instance mesh selector — heterogeneous meshes in one rasterizer would require a new compute stage (out of scope for Phase A). Camera: `position=[0,2000,5000]` looking at origin, `far=20000`. FPS overlay shows per-building tri count + total. Phase A measurement only — no production file edits. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-22 — Phase 1 + Phase 2 perf merged. **Phase 1 (Win G frustum culling correctness):** `applyFattenedFrustumCulling(root, factor=1.6)` exported from `vrm-loader.ts`. Replaces 7 defensive `traverse((o) => { o.frustumCulled = false })` calls across `arena-npcs.tsx` (×2), `arena-location-npcs.tsx`, `player-avatar.tsx`, `town-guide.tsx`, `quest-npc.tsx`, `cosmetic-loader.tsx` (×1) — all of which were overwriting the Wave 3 bind-sphere fattening that ran inside `normaliseVRM`. Helper is idempotent via `_fattenedBy` geometry tag; `normaliseVRM` now delegates to it. Three `frustumCulled = false` sites in `cosmetic-loader.tsx` are intentionally preserved (two are `THREE.Group` wrappers, one is a module-scope shared aura geometry). §6f rule 10 added. **Phase 2 (Strategy 2A material dedup):** `scripts/dedup-buildings.mjs` run on all 12 building GLBs. Outputs `*-opt1.glb` variants. `gltf-transform dedup` + custom untextured material consolidation. 142→133 materials (9 saved). Sandy-treedome: 15→11 (4 untextured vertex-color consolidated). Arcade: 34→30 (4 solid-color exact-match merged). Cove: 5→4 (1 duplicate baseColor+emissive texture deduped). Total file size: −809 KB (sandy-treedome: −662 KB). All 12 BUILDING_MODELS paths in `arena-buildings.tsx` updated to `*-opt1.glb?v=2` (`patricks-rock` + `squidward` bumped to `?v=3`). `asset-preload-manifest.ts` BUILDING_GLBS updated. `sw.js` PRECACHE_GLBS updated to current 12-building roster + version bumped to `v5`. `BuildingVisitVignette.tsx` BLDG_PATH updated. AABB extents in `world-colliders.ts` unchanged (dedup does not modify vertex geometry). Strategy 2B (atlas) excluded: 41/142 materials have UV outside [0,1] (boating-school 19/25 with max up to 65535, salty-spitoon all 3 with max ≥60000). See `docs/perf-phase2-recon-2026-05-22.md`.

Prior last edit: 2026-05-22 — NPC speech-bubble occlusion fix: `BUBBLE_Y` raised 20→150 wu in `npc-speech-bubbles.tsx`. Root cause: at Y=20 the camera→anchor ray struck the BASE of structures (shisha-oasis platform, stall counters) from above; those triangles have +Y normals, so the ray hit their back face; `material.side === FrontSide` causes Three.js Raycaster to skip back-face hits → zero intersections returned → bubble rendered over the structure. At Y=150 the anchor sits in the wall/canopy zone of every structure (all ≥500wu tall) where wall triangles have outward-facing normals that the ray hits front-on → correctly occluded. Visual bonus: bubble floats above NPC head (convention for speech bubbles in games). Prior: `npc-speech-bubbles.tsx` adds `occlude: true` to `useWorldLabel` (same 10 Hz staggered raycast against `userData.isOccluder` meshes that name tags use). Prior 2026-05-21 — React #418 hydration fix: `DeferredTerrainPreloads` and `DeferredNpcPreloads` in `game/page.tsx` converted from static imports to `dynamic({ ssr: false })`. `arena-terrain.tsx` evaluates `FORCE_WEBGL_TERRAIN` at module scope from `navigator.userAgent` — this ran on the server (where navigator is undefined → false) and then again on the client (where it may be true for non-WebGPU browsers), producing a different material type from `SandFloor.useMemo`, causing React to tear down and rebuild the client tree ("loaded twice" symptom). Safe to ssr:false because both components only fire `useGLTF.preload()` in useEffect. Prior 2026-05-21 — Squidward body-anchor U+2019 (reverted — was wrong, see above). Prior 2026-05-21 — Critical crash fix: `createWebGPURenderer` and glFactory catch block now use `THREE.WebGPURenderer` from the static top-level `import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu'` instead of `await import('three/webgpu')`. Dynamic import of `three/webgpu` inside the renderer factory caused webpack to create a second chunk with a separate module instance — `IndexNode` and other Three.js singletons from that second instance were different objects from those used by materials registered via `extend(THREE)`. When a material from instance A was compiled by a renderer from instance B, `IndexNode.VERTEX` appeared `undefined` → `SES_UNCAUGHT_EXCEPTION: TypeError: can't access property "VERTEX", yb is undefined` on Chrome/Brave/any browser without `navigator.gpu`, first-time visitors (cold cache) only. Root cause: two module instances of `three/webgpu` in the same page. Fix: use the same static import namespace throughout. Prior: 2026-05-20 — iOS Safari black-scene fix: `createWebGPURenderer` now detects iOS Safari + any browser without `navigator.gpu` and passes `forceWebGL:true` to `WebGPURenderer`. This skips the `navigator.gpu.requestAdapter()` call (which throws TypeError on iOS because `navigator.gpu` is undefined) and boots the WebGL2 backend directly. TSL node materials (`MeshBasicNodeMaterial`, `PointsNodeMaterial`, `MeshStandardNodeMaterial`) compile to GLSL via `GLSLNodeBuilder` on the WebGL2 backend — same visual output as the desktop WebGPU path. Module-scope `IOS_SAFARI` + `WEBGPU_ABSENT` + `FORCE_WEBGL` constants logged to console on init.
